=== VANILLA DRIFT, 1.20.1 -> 1.21.1, measured on the corpus ===

referenced vanilla types      3106
member references checked     25258
  still resolve               22785  (90.2%)
  SIGNATURE CHANGED           1461
  MEMBER GONE                 1012
TYPE GONE                     126

skipped, a rule handles them  33
skipped, owner type is gone   429
skipped, name still reads SRG 1  <- mapping health; want 0

Jar counts, not call sites. One mod calling something forty times is
one mod's worth of evidence.

=== SIGNATURE CHANGED (1461) ===
The member is still there and takes something else. This is Phase 4's central
problem -- a vanilla type changed shape underneath the signature. Read the two
descriptors together; the difference names the type that moved.
